# Complete shop category, item, and Blueprint class reference

[中文](../zh/14-shop-reference.md) | [JSON](../reference/shop-reference.json) | [Independent item guide](13-new-items.md)

Snapshot: 2026-10-26, current project UE5.6.1. The export covers 11 tables and 0 native item assets, with 1965 source rows. The 1650 static shop-row occurrences merge into 825 items; 157 actually registered fish-runtime items are added, producing 982 distinct ItemIds. The 158 drink-recipe rows remain separate.

The index retains 108 hidden entries, 102 entries without an item Actor class path, and 140 entries with an empty category. All 825 repeated ItemIds occur in a source table and the combined table; overlapping payloads currently agree (0 differences). Every source remains listed.

Names retain the exported FText source language. ClassPath maps the source field 物品Alass; BoxClassPath maps 货物ActorClass. Entries without an item class, such as expansion or recipe entries, are not spawnable Actor templates. A supplied path still requires host type validation. Hidden or uncategorized entries are references, not a promise that they can currently be purchased.

Fish entries come from actual host runtime registration, not inferred Function values. JSON also retains raw fish rows and registration evidence. Other mods and later generated data remain outside this snapshot. Overlapping native-asset records are retained separately rather than inventing an initialization order.

Verified templates: `1022` (桶装矿泉水 / bottled water) uses `BP_1022水桶`; `3015` (液体容器 / liquid container) uses the actual shop child `BP_设施3015_水容器_Child`. A template ID is an ItemId, not the asset name `BP_液体容器`.
